- 3 个 RUNNING 实验的 cron 完成回调

### 4. 📋 待办
- **Worker 回调到达（analyze eval_llama_sft_s43_no_evidence_r2）→** 提取 ign_f1，计算 s43 evidence tax（= with_evi ign_f1 - no_evi ign_f1），update_registry 转 DONE，汇报 Director
- **eval_llama_sft_s44_no_evidence_2048 完成 →** 对比 1024 版 ign_f1=0.3326，若 2048 版恢复到 ~0.47-0.52 → 确认截断 artifact，论文用 2048 数据；否则 s44 SFT 真的 evidence-dependent
- **eval_llama_sft_s42_no_evidence_2048 完成 →** 与 s44 2048 版 matched comparison，计算 3-seed evidence tax mean±std（s42/s43/s44 全部用公平条件）
- **s42 + s43 + s44 三组 2048 版（或 s43 1024 版如果 format_ok 率高）全部完成 →** 汇总 LLaMA SFT 3-seed evidence tax，与 RSFT 3-seed tax（-1.14pp±0.36pp）对比，填 ROADMAP 表格
- **plan017_14b_ced_scoring 完成 →** 检查产出训练样本数（预期 ~3000+），与 4B CED scoring（3014 docs, avg_f1=0.676）对比，然后开始 14B RSFT train
- **cuda:1 空闲（r2 分析完）→** 可以安排新实验，优先看 ROADMAP 中 14B pipeline 或其他待跑任务

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- **论文核心**: FREIGE — 3 个 contribution: (1) RSFT+NLI 有效性 (2) Decoupling spectrum (3) Scale-dependent evidence tax + variance compression
- **当前焦点**: LLaMA SFT 3-seed evidence tax 收集。RSFT 已完成（-1.14pp±0.36pp），SFT 需要 s42/s43/s44 三组 no-evidence eval 才能计算
- **D136 关键发现**: LLaMA SFT evidence tax 极度 seed-dependent（s42 tax=-1.65pp，s44 ign_f1=0.3326 异常低）。截断率差异巨大（s42: 22.2%, s44: 41.7%）。2048 重跑正在验证是否截断 artifact
- **D134**: evidence tax 必须用 fair comparison（format-OK 子集），这是方法论修正
- **D133**: LLaMA 升级为 co-primary evidence（采纳 Reviewer 5 建议），不再只是 supplementary
- **投稿目标**: EMNLP 2026 Main（ARR July cycle, ~56 天）
- **易错点**: 
  - s44 no-evidence 1024 版的 ign_f1=0.3326 是截断导致的异常值，**不能直接用于论文**
  - evidence tax 计算要区分 raw 和 fair（format_ok 子集），论文只用 fair
  - 14B pipeline 尚未完成（gen 62%，scoring 在跑），是 ROADMAP 表格最后一个缺口

## GPU 分配教训（2026-05-20）

LLaMA SFT 脚本里有 `export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=${GPU:-${CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES:-0}}`，会覆盖 submit_training_job 注入的 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES。**必须通过 `GPU=X` 环境变量传物理 GPU 编号**，不能依赖框架注入。

```json
{
```

```json
  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have a clearer picture. Let me summarize:

**RUNNING (1)**:
- `d140_4b_sft_s42_with_evi_2048` - cuda:3, 4B SFT with-evidence @2048

**PREPARING (3)** - but cron shows they have running processes:
- `eval_llama_rsft_s42_no_evi_2048` - PID 95606 (cron says running)
- `test_eval_14b_rsft_s42` - PID 97434 (cron says running)


- `test_eval_llama_rsft_s42` - just created 9s ago

These three are stuck in PREPARING status even though they have active processes according to cron. They were submitted via submit_training_job which created the cron entries, but the registry never got updated to RUNNING. I need to fix this sync issue.

Looking at recent completions from the D140 wave, I see `d140_4b_sft_s42_no_evi_2048_fix` finished successfully with an ign_f1 score of 0.4016, though it had to truncate 178 out of 985 samples (18.1%).

The 8B model without evidence achieved an ign_f1 of 0.5019 with essentially zero evidence tax, while the 1.7B model without evidence showed a positive evidence tax of 0.0573, suggesting evidence actually helps at that scale. The 8B and 1.7B models with evidence included both completed successfully with ign_f1 scores of 0.5012 and 0.4806 respectively.

For the Qwen matched@2048 evidence tax results, the 1.7B model shows zero evidence tax with evidence enabled.

Looking at the LLaMA SFT matched@2048 tax results across different seeds, I'm seeing negative evidence taxes ranging from -1.53pp to -11.20pp, with an average of -5.19±5.25pp, indicating evidence generally hurts performance across these configurations. I've also got some additional evaluations running in the background for LLaMA RSFT variants.

I notice there's a synchronization issue where some experiments are marked as PREPARING in the registry but actually have crons and PIDs running—this happened because the Worker submitted them via the training job endpoint but didn't update the registry status to RUNNING. I need to fix this mismatch and track down which GPUs these jobs are actually using, since the previous wave (plan017_14b_ced_scoring and the eval_llama_sft variants) has completed and freed up resources.

Now I'm seeing a new batch of experiments spawned, with d140_4b_sft_s42_with_evi_2048 already running on cuda:3, while the two RSFT eval jobs are stuck in PREPARING despite being active. I should pull the full experiment records to check their GPU assignments and spec fields, since the submit_training_job call should have populated those even if the registry status wasn't updated.
```

_source line 85 · 2026-05-21T10:37:28.433Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01CxPhU4gxeddqdhpJRwTgoX`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have the resource saturation skill. Let me prepare a report to Director.

Current state:
- GPU 4/4 in use ✓
- Worker pool: 0 running ← violation (should be ≥ 2)
- Key new results from D140 fix wave:
  - 1.7B: evidence tax = +5.73pp (evidence beneficial for small models)
  - 8B: evidence tax = -0.07pp (essentially zero)
  - 4B: pending (with-evi running)
  
I need to spawn at least 2 workers to handle non-GPU tasks. Looking at what I can parallelize, I should aggregate the current Qwen evidence tax results since I have solid data from the 1.7B and 8B runs—this is a significant finding that can inform the D142 narrative while waiting for the 4B results to complete.

Beyond that, I could run a literature check on scale-dependent evidence effects, update the paper framing with the new Qwen direction, and record the M027 fix results. But first I should check whether the D140 fix results have already been reported to the Director—the handoff seems stale given that the 1.7B no-evi fix finished with a +5.73pp tax and the 8B run also completed, both major developments since the last documented state. The evidence tax pattern across model sizes is striking: smaller models like 1.7B actually benefit from evidence (+5.73pp), while 8B shows no effect (-0.07pp), and LLaMA exhibits high variability. This suggests evidence acts as a capability amplifier for smaller models but becomes neutral or unstable at larger scales.

I need to report these findings to the Director and set up Workers to aggregate the full Qwen analysis and search for related literature on scale-dependent evidence effects.
```

```json
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：汇总 Qwen 全尺度 evidence tax 结果，更新 memory.md\n\n### 背景\nD140 波次修正了 M027 adapter 错误（之前所有 Qwen no-evi eval 误用了 plan_014 no-evidence training adapter 而非标准 SFT adapter + --no_evidence flag），现在 1.7B 和 8B 的正确 matched@2048 evidence tax 已出。\n\n### 已有数据（Qwen matched@2048 evidence tax）\n- **1.7B**: with_evi=0.4806, no_evi=0.4233, tax=+5.73pp（evidence 有益，小模型依赖 evidence）\n- **4B**: no_evi=0.4016, with_evi=运行中（exp: d140_4b_sft_s42_with_evi_2048）\n- **8B**: with_evi=0.5012, no_evi=0.5019, tax=-0.07pp（evidence 中性）\n- **14B**: 无 SFT evidence tax（只有 RSFT）\n\n### LLaMA 对比数据\n- **SFT matched@2048 tax**: s42=-1.53pp, s43=-11.20pp, s44=-2.83pp, mean=-5.19±5.25pp\n- **RSFT @1024 tax**: -1.14±0.76pp\n\n### 你要做的\n1. `read_project_file(file=\"memory.md\")` 读取当前 memory.md\n2. 在 memory.md 中找到或创建 \"Qwen Evidence Tax (D140 Fix)\" 段落，写入：\n   - 上述已有的 1.7B 和 8B tax 数据（标注 M027 修正后的结果）\n   - 4B 待完成\n   - Scale gradient pattern 解读：1.7B(+5.73pp) → 8B(-0.07pp)，evidence 从补偿到中性\n   - 对比 LLaMA 8B SFT(-5.19±5.25pp) 和 RSFT(-1.14±0.76pp)\n   - 对 D142 \"capability-dependent amplifier\" narrative 的支撑分析\n3. 如果 memory.md 中有 M027 之前的旧 Qwen evidence tax 数据，标注为 INVALIDATED\n4. `save_project_file(file=\"memory.md\")` 保存\n\n### 写入原则\n- 只写不可从 registry 派生的决策原因和 pattern 分析\n- 不要抄 raw metrics（registry 里有）\n- 重点是 pattern 解读和对论文 narrative 的影响",
```
